CONRADI LYCOSTHENIS PRODIGIORUM AC OSTENTORUM CHRONICON
Conrad Lycosthenes (1518-1561)
Basel: Henricpetri, 1557
========================================================================
PROVENANCE NOTE (Tower of Babel staging): Latin text after the 1557 Basel
first edition (BIU Santé / Internet Archive scan). Public domain. The work is
complete, ending with FINIS: the great chronicle of prodigies and portents
from the Creation to 1557 — monstrous births, sea monsters, celestial marvels.
The pivotal Renaissance collection of the prodigy tradition.
========================================================================
